Columns: icon name | glyph (paste into a Text widget) | unicode | families
Families -> Font assets: classic = FontAwesome-Pro<Weight>_Font,
  sharp = Sharp<Weight>, duotone = Duotone<Weight>,
  sharp-duotone = SharpDuotone<Weight>, brands = Brands, and each display
  family (slab, pixel, etch, ...) = its own single face.
An icon renders in a face only if its family is listed on its row.
C++ constants for the curated set: POUIIcons.h; add new ones as \uXXXX.
